# Government decisions (HG): 2022

1222 acts, 1,277 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2022.json](2022.json).

## Withdrawn from the record (14)

Acts published here and later found not to belong (for example, titled as Government decisions but issued by another body). Their folders were removed by a correction commit; the commits that added them stay in the history. Their ids are retired: a later act with the same number gets the id with `-2` appended.
